@@DOC README.md (RM)
- **Covers:** the package's master document (1745 lines): five pipelines (drawing/admittance, perception → ID → 6D pose → tracking → stationary refinement, assembly → seams/tacks (mode A) → mesh export, pen marking, hands-free operation); the repository layout; "How the pieces connect" (the one-page data flow with every file); data flows A–E (drawing, admittance, perception/bridge/click protocol/tracking, assembly/SEPC/multi-view, gestures + camera aim); the node reference with parameters and defaults; the library modules; Algorithms §1–§16 (frames, organized normals, voxel, point-to-plane ICP + normal gate, Fast-ICP/Anderson, Welsch with dynamic ν, the ICP tracker's three filters and tick optimisation, mode A seams + tackrule-0.1 + the radius-PCA fallback, drawing kinematics, admittance, sim depth noise, PPF classification, assembly mesh export, §14 placement accuracy 8 → 2.5 mm with the error budget, §15 multi-view, §16 depth calibration); interfaces, topics, TF tree, real vs twin, launch files, "Run it" (incl. the calibration routine and the gesture cycle), extending, and ~30 gotchas.
- **Status:** current overall (edited through 2026-10-16: aim relay, cable window, overshoot at the table). Partly stale inside: §15 "Acceptance (D7)" still says 10 mm / **4°** and "an overlap > 1 mm" (the node table says `mv_max_correction_mm/deg` 10/**5**; TN/TD 2026-10-15 changed the overlap rule to "not worse than the saved assembly's"); §15 overlap rule still says "its box" (true shapes since 2026-10-14); L196 "no Servo, no switching" predates the aim's controller switching; L1585 says `welding_points` stays a service call although `Open_Palm` now triggers it; §7 (ICP tracker), §8 radius-PCA and §9–§11 (drawing, admittance, sim noise) describe superseded or side pipelines; Coordinate-frames section still names `sam6d_object` (FP adds `fp_object`).
- **Decisions held (dated):** mode A default since 2026-09-21; calibrated kinematics everywhere since 2026-09-29; normal gate 2026-09-29; FP tracking default since 2026-10-06 (`tracking_source:=fp`); `transit_planner` `aps` default since 2026-10-06; `smooth_transits` default true since 2026-10-05; departure points 100 mm since 2026-10-14; `transit_budget_s` 2 s since 2026-10-14 (1 s before); tubes/swept_slab in the registry since 2026-10-09; camera aim 2026-10-15; relay 2026-10-16; overshoot ends at the table 2026-10-16; wrist_3 window 180° 2026-10-16.
- **Feeds:** every section of Ch.3 (it is the main source), 5.1–5.5, 6.1–6.6, App. A (parameters), App. C (derivations of §4–§6, §15, §16), App. D (repository layout, launch, "Run it").
@@END
@S 3.1 | Five pipelines in one package: (1) surface drawing / admittance control; (2) perception → object ID (PPF) → 6D pose (FoundationPose) → live tracking (FP on the laptop GPU, 25–29 Hz) → stationary refinement (point-to-plane ICP) and freeze; (3) assembly → weld seams and tacks (mode A) → new-model export; (4) pen marking on the UR5e, "the validation of everything above, independent of the camera"; (5) hands-free operation by gestures seen by the same RealSense — RM L7-30
@S 3.1 | The same nodes and topics run on the real robot + RealSense and on an Isaac Sim digital twin; only the joint-state source (`/joint_states` vs `/isaac_joint_states`), the camera front-end and the clock differ — RM L32-33, §Real robot vs digital twin, §Launch files
@S 3.1 | The camera extrinsic (`camera_extrinsic_tf_publisher`, static `tool0 → camera_color_optical_frame`) is "the bridge between the two halves" (perception and robot) — RM L98-101
@S 3.1 | `assembly.json` is "the hinge between perception and motion": read by `~/welding_points`, `tack_reachability.py` and `tack_marking_node.py` — RM §How the pieces connect L195
@S 3.1 | One assembly in order (the source for Fig. mc_pipeline): wrist D435i → `realsense_camera_node`; `camera_extrinsic_tf_publisher` (T_tcp_to_cam.npy) + `robot_state_publisher`; `foundationpose_bridge_node` → HTTP over Tailscale → `fp_server.py` (SAM2 mask from click → PPF names the CAD → FoundationPose pose); `icp_pose_refiner_node`: tracking (fp: `fp_tracker_node` → `fp_track_server.py` ws://127.0.0.1:5001), `~/save_object` (robust mean + ICP on 5 live clouds → SEPC + assembly.json), `~/refine_pose` (4 views at 0.4 m, median of 16 frames, joint refinement, camera translation out, ISO 5817 warning), `~/welding_points` (registry → D4 rule under pose tolerance → welding_seams.json → tackrule-0.1 → welding_tacks.json); `tack_reachability.py` (no ROS) → tack_reach.json; `tack_marking_node` `~/plan`, `~/next` (transit → force-gated descent → dot/stroke → retract) → tack_marks.json — RM §How the pieces connect L143-185
@S 3.1 | Closing the loop: `~/export_mesh` boolean-unions the saved parts' CADs at their poses (trimesh + manifold3d) into a watertight `assembly_mesh.ply` (mm) that `~/add_model` uploads, so the assembly becomes a classifiable part for the next cycle; `~/reset_environment` archives the assembly and drops the SEPC (a stale SEPC "keeps subtracting itself out of the live crop"); restarting the node is not equivalent (files reload from disk) — RM §D, §13, Run it
@S 3.1 | Hands-free: "The operator never touches a keyboard on either machine"; but `export_mesh` and `reset_environment` remain service calls ("only 7 built-in gestures and they are spent") — RM L28-30, L1583-1586
@S 3.2 | Frame chain: T_base←cam(t) = T_base←tool0(q(t))·T_tool0←cam; T_base←obj(t) = T_base←cam(t)·T_cam←obj; eye-in-hand drives three decisions: the SEPC is stored in `base_link`; TF lookups use the cloud's own timestamp, not "now"; a mask is valid only for the frame it was captured from — RM §1
@S 3.2 | The error chain checked by the pen: "camera depth → hand-eye extrinsic → kinematics → registration → seam → planner → pen tip"; rule: "measure a link against something that does not depend on it, before changing it"; references made with the robot: the pen tip (4-point TCP, then `tcp_check`), the table plane by pen touches, pen touches on the parts (`touch_probe.py`) — RM §14 L1109-1117
@S 3.3 | Calibration routine in order: 0. UR driver with `kinematics_params_file:=config/ur5e_calibration.yaml`; 1. pen TCP: the pendant's 4-point TCP → `config/pen_tool.json` `pen_tip_m` (and the pen capsule's p1); 2. table plane by pen touches (`table_touchoff.py`, empty table around the hexagon) → `notebooks/table_plane.json`; 3. camera depth: square to the table over the touched patch at ~0.5 m, `tare_distance.py` → Tare ground truth, stop the perception launch, realsense-viewer Tare, restart; 4. check depth vs range, then the tilt (4 yaws) with `extrinsic_check.py`, `refine_extrinsic_from_table.py --write`, back up and promote `T_tcp_to_cam_refined.npy`, restart, check again; 5. extrinsic translation estimated by every `refine_pose` run; `selfcal_extrinsic.py --write` once 3+ runs agree across distinct scenes — RM §Calibration routine L1517-1540
@S 3.3 | "Order matters: kinematics → pen TCP and table plane → camera depth → extrinsic rotation → extrinsic translation. A step done on top of an uncorrected earlier one learns its error (the self-calibration history recorded the depth bias as an extrinsic offset)" — RM §16 L1262-1264
@S 3.3.1 | Factory kinematic calibration `config/ur5e_calibration.yaml` used since 2026-09-29 by the URDF, the UR driver (`kinematics_params_file:=`), every planner and tool (`use_kinematics`); reports record the model; the calibrated FK reproduces the pendant TCP at every pose (0.001°), the nominal chain wanders 2.4–4.2 mm / 0.55°; marks were 3.7–4.3 mm with a model predicting 5.4/5.9 mm — RM L124, §14 29 Sep row
@S 3.3.1 | `tcp_offset_probe.py` reads the pendant's active TCP relative to `tool0` (`tcp_pose_broadcaster` vs FK), prints the `--tcp-offset` line, shows the nominal-vs-calibrated FK gap (~3 mm at the tip) — RM §Node reference L533
@S 3.3.1 | `pen_tip_touchoff.py`: 4-point TCP without the pendant (tip on one fixed point from 4+ wrist orientations, least-squares tip in tool0 + point in base_link with RMS) — a cross-check — RM L534
@S 3.3.1 | `table_touchoff.py`: from a pen-down start 3–8 cm above an empty table, touches a regular hexagon (10 cm edges) plus its centre; each touch two-speed (fast to find the table, back off 3 mm, slow touch recorded), cancelled at the touch force; tip from the driver's calibrated TCP pose; plane fit (height at centroid, tilt, RMSE over 7 points, repeatability); writes `table_plane.json`, which the real-robot launch hands to the ICP ground cut; table tilted 1.57° in base_link; releases never blocked — RM L535
@S 3.3.1 | TCP check (`table_touchoff mode:=tcp_check`, 28 Sep): one spot touched vertically at rolls 0/90/180/270 and tilted 30° toward four azimuths; least squares for the TCP error: lateral 0.21 mm; the printed −4 mm along the pen = the tilted touches' +0.54 mm × 1/(1−cos 30°): "a rounded or flexing tip, not a length error; not applied" — RM L535, §14 28 Sep row
@S 3.3.1 | Table as reference: the flat ICP ground cut at −100 mm left the table IN (up to 6 mm above the cut) under the base plate (table tilt 1.57°, 8 mm of height change across the work patch); fix: ground cut follows the measured plane (`ground_plane_file`, 2 mm offset) → marks 3–5 mm (28 Sep); the table plane is also the height reference for `extrinsic_check`, the Tare distance and the collision model — RM §14 28 Sep row, L127, L532
@S 3.3.2 | Hand-eye tools: `helper/calibration/extract_extrinsics.py` (ChArUco + RTDE capture, requires `--tcp-offset`, prints a residual judge), `resolve_handeye.py` (numpy re-solve of saved samples, leave-one-out, TCP composition, `--write`); artefacts `T_tcp_to_cam.npy` (+ `.json`: samples, TCP offset, quality), `handeye_samples.npz`, `T_tcp_to_cam_wrong.npy` (the July file that failed silently) and `T_tcp_to_cam_raw_tcpframe_*.npy` (an uncomposed solve) kept as evidence — RM L127, L129
@S 3.3.2 | 24 Sep incident 1: contacts 19–29 mm early, others in air; the stored extrinsic judged against its own samples: board-in-base range 120 mm; cause: the July OpenCV Tsai solve had failed silently, nothing printed a residual; fix: numpy Park–Martin re-solve (2–4 mm on the same samples), residual judge printed after every solve; after: depth right, 6 cm off in the table plane — RM §14 24 Sep row 1
@S 3.3.2 | 24 Sep incident 2 (TCP frame): Z right, XY off ~6 cm; re-solved camera 62 mm from the camera body seen in RViz, same radius, 37° apart around the flange; cause: the capture reads the robot at the pendant's ACTIVE TCP, the solve is the camera in that frame but ROS hangs it on `tool0`; "The residual cannot see this: a rigid change of the gripper frame is invisible to it"; fix: pendant TCP calibrated to the pen tip, recapture with `--tcp-offset`, composed into tool0; both tools now refuse to write without it; after: contact depth −0.4/−0.6 mm, lateral ~8 mm — RM §14 24 Sep row 2
@S 3.3.2 | 28 Sep: recalibrations graded BAD/FAIR and disagreed; grade fixed (rotation spread about the mean, not the worst pair vs sample 0: 6.8° → 1.8°, GOOD); `extrinsic_check.py` (yaws 180°+ apart) split the tilt: camera-fixed 0.36° (~2 mm at 300 mm) = the ChArUco solve's own rotation uncertainty (~1.8°/√33), "which is why recalibrating kept wandering"; `refine_extrinsic_from_table.py` → camera tilt 0.04°; marks 4.3 / 3.7 mm, contact depth +4.2 / +2.8 mm — RM §14 28 Sep row
@S 3.3.2 | Process lesson: "A calibration residual only says a solution fits its own samples": it missed a failed solve (never printed), a TCP composed in the wrong frame (invisible by construction) and a 0.36° rotation bias (inside its own noise); each found by comparing against an independent reference — RM §14 L1130-1133
@S 3.3.2 | `extrinsic_check.py` separates a camera-fixed tilt from the table's own when the wrist yaws span 90°+; 2 Oct: 4 yaws, camera-fixed 0.49°, table 0.44°, residual 0.05° → camera ≈ 0.07° — RM L538, §14 2 Oct row
@S 3.3.2 | The extrinsic is solved from `handeye_samples.npz` with the pendant TCP composed in; `T_tcp_to_cam.json` records that; "every registered pose, seam, tack and mark inherits its error" — RM §How the pieces connect L191-192
@S 3.3.3 | §16: Δz = z²·δ/(f·B), "grows with range squared, so a check at one distance says little"; extrinsic_check from three heights: +1.92/+0.31/−3.28 mm at 291/439/626 mm; a + k z² fit (k ≈ −17 mm/m²) predicts the middle to 0.2 mm, a line misses by 0.7 mm; δ ≈ 0.6 px; from the scan home (~0.6 m) every registration ~7 mm too low — RM §16
@S 3.3.3 | Tare: the RealSense Tare (realsense-viewer, after the on-chip calibration) "is only as good as the ground-truth distance entered"; by hand it over-corrected into a 1.1 % scale error (+1.7/+3.3/+5.2 mm at 0.29/0.42/0.60 m; the D435's depth origin sits 4.2 mm behind the glass); computed from the robot it worked: re-measure the table; camera square to the table (not the floor; table tilted 1.57°) over the touched patch at ~0.5 m; `tare_distance.py` prints the depth along the optical axis plus tilt/patch distance to adjust; stop the launch, Tare, restart; check at 0.3/0.45/0.6 m (after +0.29/+1.06/+1.43 mm = 1.1 mm across the range), then 4 yaws — RM §16
@S 3.3.3 | §14 1 Oct row: contact 8.5–9 mm early on both sides, registration OK; pen touches base top +6/+8 mm; close views see the base +4 mm, the 0.6 m view 0; extrinsic_check +1.9/+0.3/−3.3 mm at 0.29/0.44/0.63 m; cause "D435i depth too long, growing with range² (a ~0.6 px disparity offset)" — RM §14
@S 3.3.3 | Twin depth noise model σ(z) = depth_noise_std_m + depth_noise_z2_coeff·z² ("Stereo triangulation error grows with the square of range") so sim thresholds transfer; "a threshold tuned at 0.4 m is ~4× tighter than the same threshold at 0.8 m" — RM §11, Gotchas
@S 3.4.1 | Gesture node runs MediaPipe's gesture recognizer on the RealSense colour stream (not a webcam): hand and workpiece in one image → the fingertip lands on a RealSense pixel directly: "no calibration between two cameras, no coordinate mapping, no mirroring" — RM §E
@S 3.4.1 | Gesture table (fp tracking): ILoveYou idle `~/capture` / segmenting re-capture; Pointing_Up idle aim / segmenting cursor, hold still `dwell_sec` → pending point; Thumb_Down idle `~/clear_click` / commit pending as negative; Thumb_Up — / commit positive, publish, `~/trigger`; Closed_Fist held `motion_hold_sec` (2 s) → `~/refine_pose` (icp: `~/run_icp`); Open_Palm idle `~/welding_points` (icp: `~/stop_tracking`) / undo the last point; Victory `~/save_object` — RM §E table
@S 3.4.1 | Why Open_Palm got the harmless action: "an open hand is how a part is placed and false-fires - 20+ times on 2026-10-06" — RM §E table
@S 3.4.1 | Cycle with FP tracking: register (Thumb_Up) → tracker starts by itself → place → Victory save → next part → Closed_Fist held 2 s refine_pose → Open_Palm welding_points; the gesture node's `tracking_source` must match the ICP node's — RM §E L448
@S 3.4.1 | Debouncing: `GestureLatch` requires `stable_frames` identical frames and fires once on the rising edge; re-armed by neutral OR a stable run of a different gesture (Pointing_Up → Thumb_Up are one finger apart and the recognizer slides between them without reporting None); `cooldown_sec` 0.4 s; the Pointing_Up → dwell → Thumb_Up sequence puts ~1 s between two fires — RM §E, Gotchas
@S 3.4.1 | Several points per capture: Pointing_Up elsewhere keeps the previous pending point as positive, "one point per part of an assembly, so SAM2 gets them all"; Thumb_Up segments ALL points: SAM2 → PPF → FoundationPose; Open_Palm undo drops the pending point, else the last kept one — RM §Run the cycle by hand gestures
@S 3.4.1 | Occlusion: the capture gesture puts the hand in the frozen frame; keep it to the side; the window shows the frozen frame so a hand across the part is visible at once; re-freeze with ILoveYou — RM §E
@S 3.4.1 | Orientation sensitivity: MediaPipe's gesture classifier is deliberately orientation-dependent (Thumb_Up and Thumb_Down have identical geometry, differ only by orientation); trained on upright camera-facing hands; on a wrist camera "upright" moves with the arm → present the hand upright relative to the image or retrain with MediaPipe Model Maker (supports CAGO's suggested rationale for holding the camera roll in the aim) — RM §E, Gotchas
@S 3.4.1 | The current mode is drawn in the window; the status line echoes every service reply; resolution mismatch between live and frozen streams → the gesture node logs an error and suppresses the cursor — RM §E, Run it, Gotchas
@S 3.4.2 | Aim specification: gesture node publishes the fingertip pixel (with the image stamp) on `/gesture/aim` (PointStamped: u, v, gesture score in z) after `aim_frames` (3) consecutive pointing frames; filtered P loop, not a PID: One-Euro on the fingertip ray, gain 2.5 /s, deadband 5 % of the image width with hysteresis, 15°/s, 60°/s² acceleration cap, two smoothing lags; joint rates by damped least squares on the calibrated Jacobian at 125 Hz on `/camera_aim/joint_rates` — RM §E L450, §Key topics
@S 3.4.2 | Relay `aim_rate_relay` (C++): ramps linearly from one input to the next over the measured input period (8 ms delay), publishes at the UR's control rate (500 Hz) on `/forward_velocity_controller/commands`; clamps speed 0.6 rad/s and acceleration 4 rad/s²; no input for 0.1 s → ramps to zero at 2 rad/s² and goes quiet (even after a kill -9 of the aim node) — RM L514, §E
@S 3.4.2 | Aim safety (as built): deadman (no fingertip for 0.3 s → ramp to stop; any other gesture stops it); ±35° pan / ±25° tilt from home; a 0.3 s look-ahead against the collision model (table plane, fixtures, arm and pen); the branch lock; the wrist singularity; |F| > 15 N; stale joint states; starts only from a still arm with the camera within 5 cm of its home position; the gesture node does not aim while `refine_pose` runs; the aim node switches the joints back to `scaled_joint_trajectory_controller` once the arm is still; refuses to start without the relay; `dry_run` is the node default — RM §E L450, L513
@S 3.4.2 | Lower the hand to stop the aim — RM §Run the cycle
@S 3.5.1 | Click protocol: `~/capture` freezes one synced RGB-D pair and republishes its colour image on latched `/perception/frozen_rgb`; the operator picks points on THAT image; `/perception/sam2_click` JSON {stamp_ns, points [[u,v]...], labels [1,0...]} (1 object, 0 not-object); `~/trigger` POSTs the frozen frame + click; a click whose `stamp_ns` does not match the held frame is refused ("the check working: the alternative is segmenting a pixel nobody looked at"); negatives carve, at least one positive required; `~/trigger` with nothing frozen falls back to grab-and-ask-the-host (`request_timeout_sec` 300); server validates click points against the frame size (400) — RM §C, L511, L516, Gotchas
@S 3.5.1 | FoundationPose has no detector, so something must say which pixels are the object: the client sends points (normal path; server runs headless without X11) or the server opens a window on the GPU host (fallback) — RM §C "Who supplies the click"
@S 3.5.1 | `fp_server.py` (Flask, GPU host, port 5000): `POST /predict_pose` → SAM2 mask from a click → PPF classifier names the CAD → `reset_object()` to that mesh → `register()`; `POST /classify` (classification only, ~1 s); `POST /add_model` (index a new .ply into the live library and persist it); re-emits SAM-6D's on-disk format (with `obj_name`) so the ICP node needs no change; `PPF_ENABLE=0` pins a single `MESH_PATH`; pose returned as 4×4 in metres — RM L516, L511, §Algorithms intro
@S 3.5.1 | PPF classification (§12): Drost et al. 2010 via OpenCV `cv2.ppf_match_3d`; F = (‖d‖, ∠(n1,d), ∠(n2,d), ∠(n1,n2)); model summarised offline as a hash table over quantised features; scene pairs vote (generalised Hough) for model + pose; the pose is computed only to score how well each CAD explains the cloud, then discarded — FoundationPose re-estimates the pose from scratch on the chosen mesh — RM §12
@S 3.5.1 | Two guards: extent pre-filter (a model whose physical diameter is far from the cloud's is skipped, so a 1000× unit mis-scale shows as "unclassifiable"); margin + verification: every candidate's inliers counted at radius `PPF_TAU` (~8 mm, the depth noise), the winner's lead over the runner-up is the margin; `PPF_MIN_MARGIN` with `PPF_STRICT` makes the server refuse rather than guess; "Two thin plates of similar size are genuinely indistinguishable from one view"; the full score table comes back and the bridge logs it — RM §12, Gotchas
@S 3.5.1 | Library built once by `build_ppf_library.py` from every .ply in `CAD_DIR`; `/add_model` adds one incrementally (each model owns its own detector, nothing else retrains); `ppf_selftest.py` scores each model against itself to find the per-model sampling step a thin curved part needs; the CAD must be in `models/` and in the server's `CAD_DIR` under the same filename — RM §12, §Extending
@S 3.5.2 | `fp_tracker_node`: seeds from the bridge's latched detection (pose + PPF name, stamped with the registered frame), pairs RGB-D by stamp, streams the newest frame (≤ 2 in flight, raw bytes on localhost, `T_base_cam` from TF at each image stamp) to `fp_track_server.py` (ws://127.0.0.1:5001, refiner only, `track_one` from the last pose; "the 8 GB laptop GPU cannot register"); publishes every tracked frame (25–29 Hz, 70–90 ms), TF `camera_color_optical_frame → fp_object` at the image stamp, a latched CAD mesh marker, JSON status at 2 Hz; LOST = fit < 0.5 for 3 frames → one frame + the CAD silhouette as mask to the desktop's `/predict_pose`, restart from the answer, no click; reconnects by itself — RM L512, §C
@S 3.5.2 | Older ICP tracking (`tracking_source:=icp`, historical): the detection only initialises; an oriented CropBox (model AABB + `crop_margin_m`) following `current_pose` replaces the mask; ground removal (hard z-cut `ground_z_m`, not RANSAC: "plane-fitting can latch onto the workpiece"); model-based background subtraction (points within `bg_subtract_dist_m` of the SEPC dropped); Fast-ICP every tick; fitness < `lost_fitness` pauses for a re-seed — RM §7, L327-331
@S 3.5.3 | `~/save_object` (fp): averages the frames at rest (`fp_save_pose_window` 75; refused when the last pose is older than `fp_max_pose_age_s` 1 s), then `fp_save_icp`: ICP on 5 fresh live clouds, used only with fitness ≥ 0.2 and a correction ≤ 20 mm / 8°; stops the tracker and waits for the next registration; "Hold still a few seconds before saving" — RM L520, §Interfaces
@S 3.5.3 | `pose_stats.py`: `stationary_tail` (the newest run of tracked poses that describe one resting place — moving a part by hand before saving stays allowed) and `robust_pose_mean` (median translation, chordal-mean rotation, jumps rejected, spread reported → `assembly.json` `pose_stats`) — RM L559, §14 25 Sep row
@S 3.5.3 | Point-to-plane ICP (§4): E = Σ((R pᵢ + t − qᵢ)·nᵢ)²; lets the model slide along a surface; Gauss–Newton under small-angle R ≈ I + [ω]ₓ, rows Aᵢ = [pᵢ × nᵢ, nᵢ], bᵢ = −(pᵢ − qᵢ)·nᵢ, `np.linalg.lstsq`; exact rotation by Rodrigues, applied on the left T ← ΔT·T; correspondences beyond `max_corr_dist` dropped; metrics fitness = n_inliers/n_model (what `lost_fitness` gates) and inlier_rmse — RM §4
@S 3.5.3 | Normal gate (§4, 2026-09-29): model sampled from every CAD face but the camera sees only some; a hidden face finds its nearest scene point on the visible face; on an 8 mm plate a false minimum with the model straddling its two faces, lean atan(8/99); outward model normals (mesh winding) vs scene normals oriented to the camera: pair only if (R nᵢ)·mⱼ ≥ cos 60°; fitness now counts only compatible pairs (≈ 0.5 for a plate seen from one side; the old 1.0 "was the warning sign"); `normal_gate_deg` 60 on every ICP match — RM §4, L520
@S 3.5.3 | Fast-ICP (§5): Anderson acceleration on se(3), ξ = log(T·T₀⁻¹), mixing coefficients from the residual history, accepted only if the energy did not increase (monotone safeguard, else a plain step and history reset); `anderson_depth` 5 default, 0 disables — RM §5
@S 3.5.3 | Welsch kernel with dynamic ν (§6): ψ_ν(r) = 1 − exp(−r²/2ν²), IRLS weight wᵢ = exp(−rᵢ²/2ν²) (a point 3ν away keeps ~1 %); MM surrogate; ν_begin = 3.0·median(|r|), ν ← 0.5ν per stage, ν_end = 1/(3√3)·noise_floor; noise floor = median over scene points of the spread of their 7 nearest neighbours along the point's own normal; NumPy port of Zhang et al., Fast and Robust ICP (`yaoyx689/Fast-Robust-ICP`) — RM §6
@S 3.5.3 | Organized cloud normals without a KD-tree: central differences over the pixel grid, n = normalize(∂X/∂u × ∂X/∂v) flipped to face the camera (O(N)); invalid depth → NaN holes; voxel downsampling by bucket centroids (normals averaged and renormalised); the node rejects clouds with height ≤ 1 — RM §2-§3
@S 3.5.3 | The SEPC (Static Environment Point Cloud): each saved part's CAD cloud at its refined pose, held in base_link; it makes ICP blind to already-assembled parts (points within `bg_subtract_dist_m` of it are deleted from the live crop every tick, so part B's ICP cannot snap onto part A when pushed flush) — RM §D, §7
@S 3.5.4 | Registry tubes carry their base cut (flat / plane / cylinder fitted): "a pipe → tube with its base cut fitted" — RM L527 (spin settling itself is in CS/TD, not RM)
@S 3.6 | `~/refine_pose` (`multiview_service.py`): plans 4 views around the centre of the saved parts at 0.4 m (seam-region coverage, IK on the elbow-up branch, collision at the 20 mm transit clearance), drives to each ONCE (force-watched trajectories, `motion.py`), per-pixel median of 16 fresh frames + camera pose from TF, returns home, saves the capture to `multiview/<timestamp>/`; everything after is computation on saved data (`multiview_capture.refine_capture`, the same function the offline replay runs); `mv_dry_run:=true` only plans and shows; `mv_capture_here:=true` captures where the arm is; refuses to move when the extrinsic in TF is not the file it records — RM §D, L520
@S 3.6.1 | Views (§15): target = the centre of the saved parts; candidates = look-at camera poses at 0.4 m, elevation 45°/60°, azimuth every 30°, 4 rolls each; each scores the seam region (a part's surface 1–30 mm from another part) it sees: in depth range (> 0.28 m), in the field of view, incidence < 60°, not occluded by another part's box; greedy pick values a point's 1st/2nd/3rd sighting 1/0.5/0.25 and keeps directions 30° apart; IK, singularity (wrist and elbow) and collision checks run lazily only on candidates the pick wants ("planning takes seconds, not a minute"); nearest-neighbour order, transits and the way home — RM §15, L569
@S 3.6.2 | Parts take turns (block-coordinate descent, 2 rounds for the two-part cell); each scene point belongs to the part with the nearest registered surface; points within 4 mm of two parts are dropped (`ownership`); ownership recomputed between rounds — RM §15
@S 3.6.2 | One part's solve about its own centre c ("a 1° turn about the base_link origin would move it 9 mm"): Σ w_k ρ(n·(Tp − q))² + ξᵀΣ⁻¹ξ·σ² + w_o Σ max(0, −sd_c − ε)²; normal gate; Welsch ν annealed from max(3·median, p90) of |r| down to the 1.5 mm point noise σ; each view equal total weight; prior Σ: 3 mm, 1°; overlap rule: CAD points on faces next to a neighbour may not sink more than ε = 0.5 mm into it (one-sided signed distance; the README still says "its box") — RM §15
@S 3.6.3 | Measured directions from H = JᵀWJ (rotation scaled by the part's radius so both in mm): measured when σ/√λ ≤ 0.5 mm; "That has to be an absolute criterion" (the 5 % rule called the standing plate's in-plane rotation unmeasured although thousands of points pinned it) — RM §15
@S 3.6.4 | d model: an extrinsic translation error d (camera frame) shifts view v's cloud by R_v d; residual n·(Tp − q + R_v d), linear in d and in every part's correction; one least-squares problem over all owned points; d's information = Schur complement S = N_dd − N_dx N_xx⁺ N_xd; only directions with σ ≤ 0.5 mm kept; a first version registered each part to each view alone and regressed: from the TRUE poses it returned (7, 13, −15) mm for an injected (3, −2, 1) — "the points, not the per-view poses, carry the information" — RM §15
@S 3.6.4 | Online self-calibration: when |d| > 0.5 mm the run takes R_v d out of every view AND R_scan d out of every saved pose (`T_static_camera`), refines again, iterating until < 0.5 mm; correcting only the views gave a fake 8 mm fit-up change; the joint fit alone does not remove d (synthetic: recovered to 0.2 mm only by estimating d) — RM §15
@S 3.6.4 | Self-calibration across runs (`selfcal.py`, `selfcal_extrinsic.py`): each run records d and its 3×3 information under the extrinsic file's sha1; combined d = (ΣI)⁺ Σ I d, judged in each run's own determined directions; a corrected extrinsic (t − R_tc d) written only when 3+ runs agree within 1 mm; promote by hand; "Repeat captures of one scene are not independent evidence"; any run before a depth recalibration learned the depth error — RM §15, L541, Gotchas
@S 3.6.5 | Acceptance (D7) as written in §15: rejected (saved pose kept, with the reason) for a correction beyond the camera share over 10 mm / 4°, fitness < 0.2, an overlap > 1 mm, or a change of the pose between touching parts > 2 mm / 1° in directions the data do not measure; a rejected part is held at its saved pose and the others refined once more against it; an accepted part whose fit-up with a held one would change too much is kept too — "the assembly is refined together or not at all" (node table: `mv_max_correction_mm/deg` 10/5) — RM §15, L520
@S 3.6.5 | `refine_pose` reply per part: ACCEPTED / KEPT AS SAVED + why, camera offset d, fit-up gaps (ISO warning); the attempted correction and the camera share are printed; replay with `multiview_refine_offline.py --set` to see what a knob changes; `~/abort_refine` stops it — RM Run it, Gotchas
@S 3.7 | Mode A (default since 2026-09-21): `weld_method` `registration` (default) or `radius_pca` (also the automatic fallback when a saved part has no registry entry and `weld_fallback_pca` is true); each saved part's weld_generator primitive at its `pose_static`, the generator's D4 accessibility rule on the assembly; every inter-part face pair a candidate with an analytic polyline, a class (`fillet`, `butt`, `lap_toe`, `edge`), the cleared torch approach axis, and weldable or a reject reason — "the negatives are kept because the planner must avoid them"; "Its only error is the registration's" — RM §8
@S 3.7 | weld_generator (sibling repo, `weldgen_path`) supplies the seam rule (`accessibility`), the primitives (`geom`) and the tack rule (`tacks`), imported at call time — "nothing here forks it" — RM L194
@S 3.7 | Needs ≥ 2 saved objects with registry entries; otherwise radius-PCA fallback (reply starts "N welding points from ...") or fails (`weld_fallback_pca=false`) with "mode A (registration seam) unavailable: <part>: <reason>" — RM §Interfaces, §8
@S 3.7.1 | Registry CLI `build_weldgen_registry.py`: exact box → `slab`; plate with small notches/tabs → envelope `slab` (ignored fraction `envelope_deficit`, at most 10 %; the slotted `test_objv1` parts); pipe → `tube` with its base cut (flat / plane / cylinder) fitted; rounded-rect tube → closed `swept_slab`; anything else (e.g. an open band whose wall varies) → unsupported with the measured reason; `--verify` checks every entry both ways (CAD vertices on the primitive, primitive vertices on the CAD, volume) against `--accept-mm` 0.25; `hand_edited` entries kept; verified against the D34 chord budget — RM L527, L126, §8
@S 3.7.2 | Registered parts do not touch: bench ICP poses put the standing plate 8–11 mm inside the base in one run and 1–8 mm above it, tilted, in the next; the seam line is the intersection of the two face planes and invariant to the in-plane slide → each face pair judged with a pose tolerance (`weld_pose_tol_mm`, 10 mm: how far a registered face may miss the other part and still be the same joint) rather than a contact tolerance; never rejects on fit-up; every seam carries `fitup_mm` (signed distance of each member's near edge to the other's face plane: gap > 0, penetration < 0), e.g. "fillet 250mm (A:+wxB:-w) penetration 8.1..10.8mm" — RM §8
@S 3.7.2 | A member tilted end to end (2 mm into the base at one end, 11 mm above at the other) gets its seam as far as it stays within the tolerance (at 15 mm the full 250 mm, at 10 mm up to where the gap reaches 10 mm), never longer than the parts; the generator's thickness cap is replaced by a face-extent rule (each face must reach past the other's plane by more than the tolerance) so a plate's own underside cannot pair with the standing plate — RM §8
@S 3.7.2 | Stated limit: a lapping sheet thinner than the pose tolerance cannot be told from a penetrating one (toes return `member_within_pose_tol`) — laps on 2–3 mm sheet need the fiducial-board bound, T-fillets do not; once the bound is measured it replaces the 10 mm default — RM §8
@S 3.7.2 | Notched/tabbed envelope parts: the features are ignored, a seam across a notch is labelled at its nominal length, a tab that passes through the other part reads as a nominal penetration of the tab length in `fitup_mm` — RM §8
@S 3.7.3 | Pairs with a tube or a swept band are intersected as curves instead (`curved_seams.py`: closed circle / ellipse / saddle / outline seams with per-point approach axes) — RM L520
@S 3.7.4 | Fit-up gap in `refine_pose`: gap between faces resting on a neighbour (down-facing faces opposite the neighbour's) measured along the whole contact, checked against ISO 5817:2023 Table 1 no. 617 via weldgen's `root_gap_limit` with a = 0.7·t: 1.06 / 1.62 / 2.68 mm at B / C / D for 8 mm plates (`mv_quality_level`, default C); over it a WARNING, never a rejection; resting-contact pull available but off (it closed a real synthetic 3 mm gap unwarned) — RM §15
@S 3.8 | tackrule-0.1 (`weld_tacks` default true): tack length clip(4t, 10, 50) mm; spacing ceiling min(33t, 400) mm; both effective ends always tacked, evenly spaced between; t the thinnest member; each tack has `tack_no` (1-based along its seam from `p0_mm`, `n_on_seam`) and `order` (scene-wide sequence: ends first, then bisection, the two sides of a joint interleaved so heat alternates); seams oriented consistently (dominant axis positive) so both fillets of a T count from the same end; `p0_mm`/`p1_mm` bound `tack_length_mm`; `approach` = torch axis — RM §8 Tacks
@S 3.8 | Example reply: "6 tacks (seam 0: 3, seam 1: 3), 16 mm each; weld order s0#1 > s1#1 > s0#3 > s1#3 > s0#2 > s1#2" (also "2 weldable seam(s): fillet 180mm ... gap 0.0..4.8mm; rejected {'too_short': 2}") — note 16 mm = 4t for t = 4 mm, not the 8 mm plate's 32 mm — RM §8, Run it
@S 3.8 | RViz: tack segments coloured yellow = first … blue = last in weld order; labels "seam.tack_no (w<order>)" — RM §8, Key topics
@S 3.9 | Controllers: motion on `/scaled_joint_trajectory_controller/follow_joint_trajectory`; forces from `/force_torque_sensor_broadcaster/wrench` at 500 Hz (the aim alone switches to `forward_velocity_controller`) — RM L196, §E
@S 3.9.1 | `collision.py`: the arm as six capsules on `kinematics.ur5e_link_frames` (tubes displaced by the URDF's shoulder/elbow offsets); mode A parts → boxes; closed-form distances: segment–segment, golden-section segment–box, separating-axis box–box (intersection only), lowest point vs plane; `CollisionModel` (`min_distance`, `in_collision`, `is_valid`, `report`); "No full self-collision; stated" — RM L563
@S 3.9.1 | Tool model `config/pen_tool.json`: pen tip (pendant TCP), touch force 1.5 N, standoff 35 mm, collision envelope of holder + pen + camera arm, camera box at the hand-eye calibration; `tool_model_marker_node` draws holder, pen, camera arm and camera body envelopes, the tip and the calibrated optical origin + axis ("it must sit on the lens") — Milestone 1's check — RM §8 pen marking, L531
@S 3.9.1 | Parts as boxes in reachability: a pipe as 6 rotated boxes, a cut pipe as sector boxes, a band as a box chain, "within ~1 mm of the surface"; table = touched tilted plane (`table_plane.json`, unless `marking.json` `table_z_m` set); `marking.json` `fixtures` — RM L529, L532
@S 3.9.1 | C++ port `_transit_cpp` (`src/transit_cpp.cpp`): FK on the active kinematics, arm capsules, tool primitives, scene boxes, table, tool-vs-arm pairs, clearance, joint limits; built from `CollisionModel.export_spec()` so Python stays the source of truth; `test/test_transit_cpp.py` proves it equal; ~590× faster — RM L567, L532
@S 3.9.2 | `tack_reachability.py` (Milestone 3, no ROS): "can the pen reach every tack, elbow-up, without touching anything but the tack?"; for every seam tries every work-angle tilt × roll on the locked branch, keeps the combination with the largest minimum clearance over approach pose, tack pose and straight descent (then least travel); on a curved seam (2026-10-09) each tack gets its own tilt × roll (clearance up to 10 mm, then the smallest joint step); the seam reports `n_reachable/n_tacks` with the reason for every unreachable tack; `--clearance/--roll/--tilt/--table-z` overrides ("the roll override is the tool-vs-world attribution experiment") — RM L529, L564
@S 3.9.2 | Branch lock: `config/marking.json` holds the scan-home pose whose branch signs are the elbow-up lock (plus clearance, work-angle and roll grids, LIN steps); `tack_reach.py`: `branch_signature`, `same_branch`, `solve_on_branch`, `pen_axis_for` (work-angle tilt about the seam tangent), `T_tool0_for_tip` (free-roll redundancy) — RM L124, L562, L564
@S 3.9.2 | wrist_3 kept within `marking.json` `wrist3_window_deg` (180°) of home in every pose, as the 2π-equivalent; on 2026-10-16 tack-to-tack continuity wound it to −287° and the cable's pull stopped two descents with a false 1.5 N contact 84 mm above SP3 — RM L532
@S 3.9.3 | `~/plan` (from the current joints): a collision-checked transit (straight edge, else OMPL's AnytimePathShortening in C++ — `transit_planner` `aps` default since 2026-10-06: 4 RRT-Connect threads hybridized + shortcut until `transit_budget_s` 2 s (1 s before 2026-10-14), then until the first path at the latest `transit_max_budget_s` 10 s; or `rrt_connect`; APS's fallback is the C++ RRT-Connect, then the Python one); planning margin `aps_clearance_margin_m` capped at half of the end poses' room ("Invalid start/goal", 2026-10-14); collision model holds the parts, the touched tilted table plane and the fixtures — RM L532
@S 3.9.3 | Transit endpoints: every reachable tack's departure point (`marking.json` `departure_m` 100 mm out along the pen, since 2026-10-14; the 35 mm approach point when that segment is not clear); tack to tack first as the tool's straight, or slightly bowed, Cartesian line (`marking.cartesian_transit`), APS only when that is blocked; at `~/next` the planned transit is joined from the arm's actual joints when they are near its start (no re-plan) — RM L532
@S 3.9.3 | Smoothing: corners rounded by the drawing server's Catmull-Rom spline, every spline sample re-checked against the collision model (`smooth_transits` default true since 2026-10-05); timing by MoveIt's TOTG through `/compute_totg` (`v_joint_rad_s` 0.3, `a_joint_rad_s2` 0.5; every blended sample checked; a trapezoidal profile when TOTG is down or leaves free space; the log names which) — RM L532
@S 3.9.3 | `_transit_cpp.plan()`: OMPL's official APS (ROS's libompl 1.7, parallel RRT-Connect threads, hybridization, shortcutting) or one RRT-Connect, in scaled joint coordinates (path length = weighted joint travel), the GIL released while planning — RM L567
@S 3.9.3 | Gates on every motion: current joints clear and on the locked branch, first point within 20° of the current joints, 8 N aborts anything; `motion.py` adds a higher release limit for moves away from a contact and a joint-jump gate; the wrench bias is re-measured before free moves — RM L532, L566
@S 3.9.3 | TOTG (Kunz & Stilman 2012) in `totg_service_node` (C++): circular blends at the waypoints, forward/backward integration against joint velocity and acceleration limits; "The Python side only ever produces geometry; time comes from here" — RM §9, L491
@S 3.9.4 | Descent: an IK chain along the pen axis: departure → approach at `v_approach_m_s` 0.05, then `v_tip_m_s`, the last `slow_zone_m` at `v_slow_m_s`; overshoot past the registered point ends at the table plane (2026-10-16); "descent at 20 mm/s cancelled at 1.5 N on the wrench" (FollowJointTrajectory goal cancelled; "the joints at the cancel = the contact"); wrench bias measured at the approach point — RM L532, §8 Milestone 4, L181
@S 3.9.4 | Contact on rigid parts is a force problem: the pen is not sprung; at 10 mm/s the stopping distance alone turned a 1.5 N detection into ~10 N on the table, and a 1 mm stroke press read 8 N on steel → the two-speed approach (last 6 mm at 2 mm/s), releases the abort force can never block, press 0, a stroke depth gain far below 1/stiffness — RM §14 L1134-1138
@S 3.9.4 | Mark: a dot (dwell) or, with `stroke_mode:=tack|seam`, a stroke (`tack` = each tack's segment, `seam` = the whole weldable seam; on a curved seam each tack draws its own piece of the curve in both modes): contact-referenced depth (measured contact + press) in 10 mm chunks, depth corrected between chunks from the force along the pen; retract along the pen; `tack_marks.json` records the tip at contact, the depth along the pen axis and per-chunk stroke forces ("the placement check the pipeline is validated by"); every run archived in `marks_history/` with its assembly; `dry_run:=true` default — RM L532, §8
@S 3.9.4 | Gotcha: contact early on BOTH sides of a standing plate = a vertical error (camera depth, something under a part); a sideways shift makes one side early, the other late; 1 mm of height ≈ 1.4 mm of contact along a 45° pen — RM Gotchas
@S 3.9.4 | Gotcha: transit aborts at ~8 N with nothing touching = F/T zero drift (7 N at rest after a power cycle); free moves re-measure the bias; check the payload on the pendant and `zero_ftsensor` — RM Gotchas
@S 3.10.1 | Node set (source for fig:architecture): `robot_state_publisher`; `camera_extrinsic_tf_publisher`; `realsense_camera_node` (or `realsense_sim_camera_node`); `depth_image_proc::PointCloudXyzrgbNode` (organized XYZRGB); `foundationpose_bridge_node`; `fp_tracker_node`; `detection_marker_node`; `icp_pose_refiner_node` (tracking display, save, SEPC, refine_pose, welding_points, export_mesh, reset; MultiThreadedExecutor with three callback groups; shared state under a lock); `gesture_control_node`; `camera_aim_node`; `aim_rate_relay` (C++); `tack_reach_marker_node`; `tool_model_marker_node`; `tack_marking_node`; `totg_service_node` (C++); CLIs `tack_reachability.py`, `build_weldgen_registry.py`, `contact_error.py`, `multiview_refine_offline.py`, `selfcal_extrinsic.py`, `compare_registrations.py`; probes `tcp_offset_probe`, `pen_tip_touchoff`, `table_touchoff`, `touch_probe`, `check_registration`, `extrinsic_check`, `tare_distance`, `pose_jitter_probe`, `aim_bag_report`; superseded `sam6d_bridge_node`/`server.py`; drawing stack — RM §Node reference, §Repository layout
@S 3.10.1 | Services are the pipeline's steps: bridge `~/capture`, `~/trigger`, `~/clear_click`, `~/add_model`; ICP node `~/run_icp`, `~/stop_tracking`, `~/start_tracking`, `~/save_object`, `~/refine_pose`, `~/abort_refine`, `~/welding_points`, `~/export_mesh`, `~/reset_environment`; marking `~/plan`, `~/next`, `~/all`, `~/home`, `~/abort` — RM §Interfaces, L532
@S 3.10.1 | Files of one assembly (runtime state in `scripts/foundationpose_results/`): rgb/depth/camera.json → detection_pem.json, detection_ism.npz, mask.png, vis_pose.png, object_name.txt → static_env.ply/.npy + assembly.json → assembly_mesh.ply → welding_seams.json, welding_tacks.json, welding_points.npy/.ply → tack_reach.json → tack_marking_plan.json, tack_marks.json; multiview/<ts>/ captures; previous_assemblies/<ts>/ archive — RM §How the pieces connect table
@S 3.10.1 | Packaging: ament_cmake with rosidl interfaces; Python library modules installed by an explicit `install(FILES)` list (not `ament_python_install_package()`), node scripts by `install(PROGRAMS)` and must be chmod +x; on 2026-10-01 the ICP node died at start-up on a new import (it now starts without `refine_pose` and says why) — RM L114, Gotchas
@S 3.10.2 | C++ components: `totg_service_node` (MoveIt TOTG), `admittance_control_jacobian_node` (drawing stack), `aim_rate_relay` (500 Hz), `_transit_cpp` (pybind11: collision model + OMPL APS/RRT-Connect, GIL released) — RM §Repository layout, L514, L567
@S 3.10.2 | Python performance work on the (historical) ICP tracker tick: ~74 ms → ~23 ms on a 640×480 cloud with bit-identical output: (1) image-space ROI crop (`roi_crop`; intrinsics inferred from the organized cloud) 38 ms → 5 ms; (2) one `NNIndex` per tick (Open3D `core.nns` 0.31 ms vs cKDTree 1.02 ms per query; cKDTree `workers=-1` slower); (3) cached Welsch ν floor (`noise_floor_refresh` 60 ticks; re-deriving it cost ~2.5 ms per frame); ICP is ~80 % of the tick, ~43 NN queries per tick — RM §7
@S 5.1 | Real D435i front-end streams colour + depth aligned to colour, publishes CameraInfo, writes live intrinsics to camera.json; depth 16UC1 mm; launch args `camera_serial_no`, `camera_width`, `camera_height`, `camera_fps` — RM L508, §Launch files
@S 5.1 | F/T: `/force_torque_sensor_broadcaster/wrench` at 500 Hz; payload must be right on the pendant (`zero_ftsensor`) — RM L196, Gotchas
@S 5.1 | Holder CAD: tube r 17.4 mm; a 42 mm capsule had cleared a 90° corner by 1.1 mm, the CAD envelope 15.5 mm — RM §14 29 Sep row
@S 5.2 | ROS 2 Jazzy; `ros-jazzy-depth-image-proc`; `colcon build --packages-select admittance_control --symlink-install`; MediaPipe recognizer bundle `gesture_recognizer.task` (float16/1 from storage.googleapis.com) + `pip install mediapipe`; optional Open3D/SciPy accelerators (pure NumPy fallback); trimesh + manifold3d for export; OMPL (ROS's libompl 1.7); pybind11; pose server = Flask in Docker on the GPU host reached over Tailscale (port 5000); laptop FP container ws://127.0.0.1:5001; Isaac Sim twin `world/welding_world.usda`; tests `.venv/bin/python -m pytest test/` — RM §Run it, L567, L130, L516

@S 5.4 | Validation instruments: `tack_marks.json` (tip at contact, depth along the pen, per-chunk forces); `contact_error.py` (tube-on-plate: fits the real tube's sideways offset and the real plate's height offset to the contacts, prints each mark's distance from the real root; "Evaluation only - never fed back"; 2026-10-14: C1 8.8 mm / E2 8.1 mm sideways, plates 1–3 mm high); `touch_probe.py` (freedrive the tip onto a surface: vs table plane, nearest registered face (+ = real surface outside), tacks); `check_registration.py` (registration vs live cloud per face: median offset and drift — blind to calibration errors) — RM L528, L536-537
@S 5.5 | Procedure details: part A trigger → (run_icp/track) → save; part B placed against A → trigger → save; right after saving `check_registration.py` (parts untouched, robot at the scan pose), then `refine_pose`; then `welding_points`; then reachability and marking; export + add_model; `reset_environment` before the next assembly ("Do not skip the reset") — RM §Assemble two parts
@S 5.5 | "Registered, then marked hours later? Check right after registering and mark right after the check: a 2 h gap left a 4 mm disagreement" — RM Gotchas

@S App.A | Parameters with defaults (RM node table / §15 / §E / §8): normal_gate_deg 60; anderson_depth 5; max_iter 30; noise_floor_refresh 60; fp_save_pose_window 75; fp_max_pose_age_s 1 s; fp_save_icp fitness ≥ 0.2, correction ≤ 20 mm / 8°; fp_display_hz 10; FP LOST fit < 0.5 × 3 frames; mv_view_distance_m 0.4; mv_n_views 4; mv_n_frames 16; mv_quality_level C; mv_max_correction_mm/deg 10/5; prior 3 mm / 1°; ε 0.5 mm; ownership dead band 4 mm; point noise 1.5 mm; observability 0.5 mm; online selfcal |d| > 0.5 mm; selfcal 3+ runs within 1 mm; views 0.4 m, elev 45°/60°, azimuth 30°, 4 rolls, depth > 0.28 m, incidence < 60°, sightings 1/0.5/0.25, 30° apart, seam region 1–30 mm, view transit clearance 20 mm; weld_pose_tol_mm 10; registry --accept-mm 0.25; envelope_deficit ≤ 10 %; tack length clip(4t,10,50), spacing min(33t,400); departure_m 0.1; approach/standoff 35 mm; v_approach_m_s 0.05; descent 20 mm/s; last 6 mm at 2 mm/s (§14); touch 1.5 N; abort 8 N; stroke chunks 10 mm; wrist3_window_deg 180; transit_budget_s 2 s; transit_max_budget_s 10 s; 4 APS threads; v_joint_rad_s 0.3; a_joint_rad_s2 0.5; first point within 20°; edge_resolution 0.01 rad; aim aim_frames 3, gain 2.5 /s, deadband 5 % width, 15°/s, 60°/s², deadman 0.3 s, ±35°/±25°, look-ahead 0.3 s, |F| 15 N, start within 5 cm; relay 0.6 rad/s, 4 rad/s², 0.1 s → 2 rad/s²; gesture cooldown_sec 0.4 s, motion_hold_sec 2 s; PPF_TAU ~8 mm; request_timeout_sec 300 — RM (sections named)
@S App.C | Derivations available in RM: point-to-plane Gauss–Newton with small-angle linearisation and Rodrigues (§4); Anderson acceleration on se(3) with the monotone safeguard (§5); Welsch MM/IRLS with ν annealing and the noise floor (§6); organized-cloud normals (§2); DLS IK Δq = Jᵀ(JJᵀ + λ²I)⁻¹e (§9); admittance z̈ = ((f_t − f_m) − Dż − Kz)/M (§10); range-squared depth noise (§11, §16); Schur-complement information of d (§15) — RM §2-§16

@@DOC notes/multiview_refine_plan.md (MV)
- **Covers:** the design and step-by-step build of `~/refine_pose` (written 2026-09-29, built 2026-10-01/02): why close views help (depth error ∝ range², camera-side errors turn with the view); the one-call pipeline; design D1 (shared `TrajectoryExecutor`), D2 (view planner), D3 (capture: settle, median of 16 frames), D4 (preprocessing), D5 (refinement of touching parts in four layers: ownership + dead band, prior, one-sided overlap + rounds, fit-up gap and absolute observability, view weights), D6 (per-view diagnostics, extrinsic estimate), D7 (accept/persist/show), D8 (safety); implementation steps 1–8 with their checks, deviations and synthetic findings; parameters; the 2026-10-01 decisions.
- **Status:** design and synthetic results current; several numbers superseded by later docs: D7 angle limit 4° (now 5°, TD 2026-10-15; node table RM L520), overlap rule "> 1 mm" (now "not worse than the saved assembly's", TN/TD 2026-10-15), `mv_observable_ratio` 0.05 in the parameter list (replaced by the absolute 0.5 mm rule, Layer 3b), `mv_dry_run` listed as `true` in Parameters but D8 says default FALSE since 2026-10-01, neighbours as face planes/boxes (true shapes since 2026-10-14, TN/TD). Step 8 (FoundationPose per view) not built. Open items moved to TD.
- **Decisions (2026-10-01):** one centre target; 0.40 m view distance (D435i minimum 0.28 m); parts take turns (block coordinate descent, `mv_rounds` 2); self-calibration yes (step 7, done first by the user's choice); FP per view later (step 8). 2026-10-02: 16 frames (was 8); Layer 3b gap warning; absolute observability.
- **Feeds:** 3.6.1–3.6.5, 3.7.4, 6.3 (synthetic and bench numbers), App. A (parameters), App. C (Schur complement, prior as MAP).
@@END
@S 3.6 | Motivation (2026-09-29): marks 5–8 mm off; pen touches split that into 1–3 mm terms (pen length, base-plate height and tilt, ear faces), each at the D435i's own level for a single scan from ~0.5 m; two sensor properties make close views worth trying: depth error grows ~ with the square of the distance; camera-side errors turn with the view (the extrinsic's translation turns with the wrist, the depth bias points along each line of sight) — MV §Why
@S 3.6 | One call does everything: plan the views, move to each, capture, refine every saved part, show in RViz, rewrite `assembly.json` and the SEPC, return to the scan home (with the home move that unwinds the wrist); reply: correction per part (mm, deg), spread between views, extrinsic estimate; `welding_points` then runs unchanged — MV §Where it sits, D7
@S 3.6 | D1: the marking node's `_execute` became `admittance_control/motion.py` `TrajectoryExecutor` (FollowJointTrajectory to the scaled JTC, wrench watchdog aborting at `abort_force_n` 8 N on every move, max joint-jump check on the first point, dry run, controller result); both nodes use it; the ICP node adds an action client, a wrench and a joint-state subscription in their own callback groups (6 executor threads) — MV D1, step 1/5
@S 3.6.1 | D2 view planner (`multiview.py`, pure, unit-tested): target = centre of the combined bounding box of the saved parts (one target, decided 2026-10-01); candidates = look-at camera poses at 0.40 m, elevation 45–60° from the table, azimuth every 30°, roll in 90° steps, camera +z at the target; camera pose → tool0 pose through the inverse of `T_tcp_to_cam`; feasibility: IK on the locked elbow-up branch (`solve_on_branch`, `home_q`), clear of the collision model (tool with camera body, saved parts as boxes, table) at `transit_clearance_m`, target depth 0.28–0.6 m; score: model points on faces next to weldable seams facing the camera within 60° incidence and not occluded (ray test), greedy pick of K = 4 views maximising joint coverage; order: nearest-neighbour in joint space from the current joints, transits by `marking.transit_path`; fewer than 2 feasible views → refuse — MV D2
@S 3.6.1 | As built (step 2, 2026-10-01): visibility first (cheap), then a lazy greedy pick so IK and collision run only on candidates the pick wants (~12 of 96); among rolls of a direction the least joint travel from home; extra rejections: elbow nearly straight (a far-side view at 0.78 m reach came out at elbow −0.16 rad), joints beyond ±2π after unwrapping (a plain unwrap put wrist_3 at −7.97 rad → `nearest_in_limits`); coverage reported against what any candidate can see (67 % of the seam band seeable at all, since the base's underside below the ear is in the band); 6 tests — MV step 2
@S 3.6.1 | The planner's 30° elevation option is off: a 30° view would halve the weakest std (0.16 → 0.08 mm) but none is reachable at 0.4 m on the bench (IK or a stretched elbow) and the 30° candidates doubled the planning time; 75° does not help (ear faces seen at > 60° incidence); from 0.4 m at 45–60° the base's 8 mm edges are never seen (> 60° incidence), their slide becomes measured only with additional 20° views; low views alone see no top face (fitness 0.04) and are rightly rejected — MV step 7, step 3 findings
@S 3.6 | D3 capture per view: settle (joint speed near 0, then 0.3 s more); `n_frames` 16 (8 until 2026-10-02) organized clouds, per-pixel median ignoring invalid pixels ("cuts the temporal noise about 3× before any geometry"); camera pose from TF at the cloud's stamp; store {q, T_base_cam, xyz_median, stamp} in `multiview/view_<k>.npz` for offline replay; tracking paused for the whole run and restored afterwards — MV D3
@S 3.6 | D4 preprocessing: crop to the saved parts' combined bounding box + 30 mm; table-plane cut (`ground_plane_file`); voxel `mv_voxel_m` 3 mm; normals in the camera frame oriented towards that view's camera (the normal gate relies on it); transform to base_link — MV D4
@S 3.6 | Capture format: `<results>/multiview/<timestamp>/` with `capture.json` (time, extrinsic file and sha1, kinematics, table-plane file, per-view angles / q / T_base_cam), `view_<k>.npz` (organized cloud in the CAMERA frame so a capture can be replayed with another extrinsic), `assembly.json` (parts at capture time); each replay ~6 s — MV step 4
@S 3.6.2 | D5 framing: "No robot touching anywhere in this step. 'Touching' here means parts touching each other"; the danger is not a gross jump (refinement starts from the saved pose, right to ~3 mm, no global search) but a quiet slide of a few mm toward a neighbour or along an unmeasured direction; four layers guard against it — MV D5
@S 3.6.2 | Layer 1 ownership: each scene point belongs to the part whose registered surface is nearest (as in `check_registration.py`); dead band `mv_dead_band_m` 4 mm (points within 4 mm of two parts dropped: the strip where parts meet pulls both ways and the root is defined by the faces either side); fixed within a round, recomputed between rounds; the normal gate separates parts meeting at a right angle (base-top normal up vs ear face sideways: 90° apart, gate allows 60°); lap joints are the hard case → half-gap matching cap (`parallel_gap_rule`), OFF by default since no swap was found — MV D5 Layer 1, step 3 deviations
@S 3.6.2 | Layer 2 prior: each part minimises the ICP energy + ξᵀΣ⁻¹ξ (ξ ∈ se(3) correction from the saved pose; Σ diagonal, `mv_prior_sigma_mm` 3, `mv_prior_sigma_deg` 1): "a maximum-a-posteriori estimate with the saved pose as the prior"; in Gauss–Newton one extra term (prior information added to AᵀA, pull to the right-hand side); "where the views constrain the pose strongly, the data wins. Where they do not, the part stays put" — MV D5 Layer 2
@S 3.6.2 | Layer 3 one-sided overlap: `mv_overlap_samples` 200 points on faces touching a neighbour; signed distance to the neighbour's surface (face planes, exact for the plate parts); below −`mv_penetration_tol_m` (0.5 mm) penalised quadratically with `mv_overlap_weight`; a gap costs nothing ("the fit-up gap is real information"); rounds (block coordinate descent) until no part moves more than 0.2 mm and 0.05°, at most `mv_rounds` 2 (two parts; decided 2026-10-01); order within a round: the part with the most owned points first (usually the base); one combined 6N solve kept as an open question — MV D5 Layer 3
@S 3.6.2 | Layer 4: each view gets equal total weight (Welsch weights multiply it) — MV D5 Layer 4
@S 3.6.2 | Deviations as built: own Gauss–Newton solver `refine_part` (the prior must act about the part's own centre: a 1° turn about the base_link origin moves the part ~9 mm; the overlap rule is a second kind of residual), reusing `NNIndex`, the normal gate and Welsch weights; robust weighting starts at max(3 × median, 90th percentile) of the residuals (with the median alone the few faces that see a slide — a plate's end faces — were weighted away before the first step) — MV step 3 deviations
@S 3.6.3 | Observability as first designed: eigen-decomposition of AᵀWA without the prior, directions below `mv_observable_ratio` (0.05) of the largest reported "not measured, held at the prior" (into `assembly.json`); replaced 2026-10-02 by the absolute rule `observable_max_sigma_mm` 0.5 "from the information of the data AND the contacts" — MV D5 Layer 2, Layer 3b
@S 3.6.4 | D6 first design: per part, register each view alone and fit t_i ≈ t0 + R_tool_i·d ("the 180° test, now done automatically on every run"), report only; replaced: d estimated from POINTS (`multiview_refine.estimate_extrinsic`: every part's correction about its own centre, no prior, and d together, residual n·(Tp − q + R_cam d)); the per-view regression returned (7, 13, −15) mm for an injected (3, −2, 1) even from the true poses — MV D6, step 7
@S 3.6.4 | "Determined" is absolute: d's information is the Schur complement with the parts eliminated; a direction counts when its std ≤ 0.5 mm (`extrinsic_max_sigma_mm`), others named and set to 0; the vertical shift (every view's cloud up/down) is the weakest direction: for views at one elevation it is indistinguishable from all parts sitting higher; the planned T views (45/60°) still measure it to 0.16 mm; a "5 % of the largest eigenvalue" rule had thrown it away — MV step 7
@S 3.6.4 | Online self-calibration (`RefineConfig.online_selfcal`, default on; `online_selfcal_min_mm` 0.5): when d exceeds 0.5 mm, R_cam d is taken out of every view and the refinement runs again; the history receives the ORIGINAL d; with it off, a d that large rejects every part; why: with d = (3, −2, 1) on rendered data the uncorrected joint fit tilted the base by a wrong 1.2° and was ACCEPTED (signs only in diagnostics: rms 0.9 vs 0.35 mm, single views spread 4.4 mm) — MV step 4 findings
@S 3.6.4 | Self-calibration (step 7): sign: an error Δ in tool0 appears as R_cam d with d = R_tcᵀΔ, so the correction is t − R_tc d (a chain test confirms); each run stores d and its 3×3 information (1/mm²); combined d = (ΣI)⁺ΣId (a direction one run can't see is filled by another); agreement judged per run within its own determined directions; runs with another extrinsic (sha1) or nothing determined skipped; write `T_tcp_to_cam_selfcal.npy` + sidecar when ≥ 3 runs agree (median moves < 1 mm leaving any one out, spread < 1 mm); promote by hand; the rotation stays the table refinement's job — MV step 7
@S 3.6.5 | D7 rejection rules (as designed/built 2026-10-01/02): correction > `mv_max_correction_mm` 10 or `mv_max_correction_deg` 4 (3 until 2026-10-02); fitness < 0.2; the face check fails; two parts overlap by more than `mv_max_penetration_mm` 1 (refined poses, exact face planes); the pose between two touching parts changed by more than `mv_max_relative_mm` 2 or `mv_max_relative_deg` 1 when that direction is not measured ("That relative pose is the fit-up"); fallback: a rejected part held at its saved pose and the others refined once more against it; an accepted part whose fit-up with a held one would change beyond the limit is kept as saved too ("refined together or not at all") — MV D7, step 3 deviations
@S 3.6.5 | Note for real replays: the 2 mm fit-up rule (`max_relative_mm`) rejected a legitimate correction in a test whose saved poses were off ~3 mm independently; "Tune it on real data, where saved poses share one scan's error" — MV step 7 note
@S 3.6.5 | D7 persist: replace `pose_static`, rebuild the SEPC, `assembly.json` `refine` block per object (pose_before, n_views, correction_mm/deg, view spread, extrinsic estimate, face check); RViz: SEPC and TFs republished, latched `/perception/icp/multiview_cloud` (stacked views coloured by view) and `/perception/icp/multiview_views` (frusta, view numbers); a run is recorded for the self-calibration only if it determines a direction of d — MV D7, step 5
@S 3.6 | D8 safety: `mv_dry_run` plans and shows without moving, default FALSE since 2026-10-01 (user's request); every move collision-planned at `transit_clearance_m` 20 mm and watched at 8 N; `~/abort_refine` stops and leaves the poses untouched; refuses with no saved part, no joint states, or an unreachable controller; before motion the extrinsic in TF must equal `mv_extrinsic_path` (0.1 mm) and the calibrated kinematics must be loaded; a failed/aborted move stops where it is with no recovery motion — MV D8, step 5
@S 3.6.4 | `--record` appends d to the self-calibration history only when the capture's extrinsic sha1 matches the file in use; `--write` writes `<capture>/assembly_refined.json` (the live assembly.json is never touched) — MV step 4
@S 3.7.4 | Layer 3b (2026-10-02): faces resting on a neighbour (down-facing faces opposite a neighbour's face) get their gap measured along the whole contact after refinement; checked against ISO 5817:2023 no. 617 at `mv_quality_level` C via weldgen's `root_gap_limit` (a = 0.7 t: 1.06/1.62/2.68 mm at B/C/D for 8 mm plates); over the limit a WARNING, never a rejection; `mv_resting_contact` default OFF (closed a synthetic 3 mm gap to < 1 mm unwarned; not needed with the absolute observability rule) — MV Layer 3b
@S 5.4 | Bench validation recipe (step 6): `touch_probe` on the base top ×2 and the ear faces ×2 before and after `refine_pose` (that day: +2.3/+4.3 and +0.9/−1.2 mm), then mark and record contact depths and mark errors; compare single scan vs multi-view — MV step 6
@S App.A | MV parameters: `mv_view_distance_m` 0.40; `mv_n_views` 4; `mv_elevations_deg` [45, 60]; `mv_azimuth_step_deg` 30; `mv_max_incidence_deg` 60; `mv_n_frames` 16; `mv_voxel_m` 0.003; `mv_max_corr_m` 0.010; `mv_max_correction_mm` 10; `mv_max_correction_deg` 4 (now 5); `mv_dead_band_m` 0.004; `mv_prior_sigma_mm` 3; `mv_prior_sigma_deg` 1; `mv_overlap_samples` 200; `mv_penetration_tol_m` 0.0005; `mv_rounds` 2; `mv_max_penetration_mm` 1; `mv_max_relative_mm` 2; `mv_max_relative_deg` 1; `online_selfcal_min_mm` 0.5; `extrinsic_max_sigma_mm` 0.5; settle 0.3 s — MV §Parameters, D3, step 4/7
@R 6.3 | View plan on the 2026-10-01 bench T: 4 views at 400 mm (azimuths 60/300/0/240, elevations 45/60); 99 % of the seeable seam region seen once, 49 % by two or more views; 26 s including the transits and the way home; ~12 of 96 candidates evaluated; 67 % of the seam band seeable at all; single plan — MV step 2
@R 6.3 | Synthetic touching-parts tests (step 3, 2026-10-01): no camera error: base tilt 0.40° → 0.02°, base height 2.25 → 0.18 mm, ear position across its face (weld root) → 0.01 mm; base in-plane slide and ear's slide along its length unmeasured (held); d = (3, −2, 1) mm: estimate (2.94, −2.17, 1.20), single views scatter > 1 mm, joint fit would change fit-up 7 mm → kept; after self-cal both accepted within 0.5 mm; overlap: ear started 2 mm into the base ends ≤ 0.5 mm deep, a real 1 mm gap kept; rounds: 2 suffice, base-first vs ear-first within 0.3 mm; lap joint: no swap even without ownership at 10 mm (half-gap cap would have blocked a 6 mm correction); synthetic — MV step 3
@R 6.3 | Synthetic captures (step 4, rendered bench T, 4 planned views): no camera error: both parts within 0.09 mm / 0.08° of truth (saved 1.9–3.5 mm / 0.3° off), d = 0.01 mm; d = (3, −2, 1): estimated (2.99, −1.99, 1.00), both parts within 0.09 mm / 0.03° (online correction); uncorrected: wrong 1.2° base tilt ACCEPTED, rms 0.9 vs 0.35 mm, single-view spread 4.4 mm; synthetic — MV step 4
@R 6.3 | d observability: vertical direction measured to 0.16 mm by the 45/60° views; over 4 noise seeds and 2 offsets the actual errors were 0.2–0.33 mm; a 30° view would make it 0.08 mm; synthetic — MV step 7
@R 6.3 | Self-calibration synthetic: 3 runs of the T with an extrinsic error (2, −3, 1.5) mm in tool0 → corrected extrinsic within 0.5 mm of the true one; synthetic — MV step 7
@R 6.3 | Live test 2026-10-01 (scratch node `icp_mv_test`): dry run planned 4 views from the live joints; `capture_here` captured 907k valid pixels, saved, refined, applied and did NOT record a 1-view run; test-suite counts: motion 7 tests (58 passed + 1 skip), views 6, touching parts 8 (72 passed), capture 6 (84 passed), selfcal 6 — MV steps 1-7
@R 6.3 | Bench before refine (step 6 baseline, 2026-10-02 morning): touch_probe base top +2.3/+4.3 mm, ear faces +0.9/−1.2 mm; after the Tare + tilt refinement + refine_pose: every tack within ~2.5 mm (single scan 3–8 mm) — MV step 6
@O 3.6 | Step 8: FoundationPose per view as a second, independent measurement (use only if FP's error is smaller than or independent of the ICP's) — NOT BUILT (decided 2026-10-01 "later") — MV step 8, TD
@O 3.6.2 | One combined solve (6N unknowns) instead of turns — only if rounds oscillate/depend on order; not needed so far (order within 0.3 mm) — OPEN — MV D5, Decisions 3
@O 3.6.4 | Self-calibration bench check (needs distinct scenes; do not promote the runs so far) — OPEN — MV step 7, TD
@O 3.6.5 | Tune `max_relative_mm` (2 mm) on real data — OPEN/unclear — MV step 7 note
@O 3.6 | Per-view depth bias not injected in the synthetic tests ("Not covered yet") — OPEN — MV step 3
@C L337-340 | close views of the joints, all poses refined together — MV §Why, D2, D5
@C L344-347 | view-planning TODO contents (targets, range/FoV/incidence/occlusion, greedy, IK/branch/singularity/collision, order and transits) — MV D2, step 2
@C L352-358 | correction about the part's own centre; prior; one-sided overlap penalty with ε — MV D5, step 3 deviations
@C L363 | block coordinate descent — MV D5 Layer 3 ("This is block coordinate descent")
@C L364-365 | ownership with a dead band (4 mm), two rounds — MV D5
@C L368-374 | absolute 0.5 mm observability; unmeasured directions held at the prior — MV Layer 2/3b, step 7
@C L379-389 | R_v d model, Schur complement, removal from views; joint fit alone does not cancel d — MV step 3/4/7
@C L403-405 | 10 mm limit; fitness 0.2; relative change 2 mm/1° — MV D7 (angle 4° there, 5° now)
@X L355-356 | ch3 Eq. mv_objective writes the overlap penalty with "the signed distance of contact sample c to its neighbour ... true shapes" — MV (2026-10-01) defines it against the neighbour's face planes (exact for plates); the true-shape signed distance came 2026-10-14 (TN/TD, `part_shapes.py`) — fine, but cite TN/TD for the true shapes and MV for the rule; ε = 0.5 mm (`mv_penetration_tol_m`) and the 200 samples are not in ch3 — MV D5 Layer 3
@X L370-372 | ch3 observability: "H = JᵀWJ ... σ_dir = σ_noise/√λ ≤ 0.5 mm" — MV/RM: rotation directions scaled by the part's radius so all are in mm, and (Layer 3b) the information includes the contacts; also the same 0.5 mm absolute rule decides d's determined directions (`extrinsic_max_sigma_mm`) — MV Layer 3b, step 7; RM §15
@M 3.6 | D3/D4 capture and preprocessing (settle 0.3 s, median of 16 frames ≈ 3× less temporal noise, crop + 30 mm, table cut, 3 mm voxel, normals towards each camera, capture stored in the camera frame for replay with another extrinsic) are not in ch3 — 3.6 — MV D3/D4
@M 3.6.1 | Practical view-planning failures (stretched elbow, wrist_3 −7.97 rad after unwrap → `nearest_in_limits`), 67 % seeable coverage, 26 s plan, why no 30°/75° views — 3.6.1 / 6.3 — MV step 2, step 7
@M 3.6.2 | The "four layers" framing (ownership, prior as MAP, one-sided overlap + turns, view weights) is a clean structure for 3.6.2 — MV D5
@M 3.6.4 | The online-vs-history distinction (history keeps the ORIGINAL d; recorded only if a direction is determined; sha1 key) and the sign convention (t − R_tc d) — 3.6.4 / App. C — MV step 4, 7
@M 3.6.5 | The fallback rule "refined together or not at all" — 3.6.5 — MV D7
@M 6.3 | The synthetic validation ladder (no error → injected d → self-cal from 3 runs) with its numbers is ready-made content for 6.3 — 6.3 — MV steps 3, 4, 7
@@DOC notes/realtime_fp.md (RF)
- **Covers:** the plan (written 2026-10-02) and status (2026-10-06) of live FoundationPose tracking: the desktop-registers / laptop-tracks split, the built files (`fp_track_server.py`, `fp_tracking.py`, `fp_stream.py`, benchmarks, `fp_tracker_node.py`), the laptop measurements, the ICP-node hand-over (`tracking_source: fp`), the speed design S1–S11 (benchmark first, server-side state with ≤ 2 frames in flight, WebSocket binary stream, ROI crop, cheap encoding, fewer refiner passes, ego-motion cancellation, TF per frame + latched mesh, cheap LOST detection, one part at a time, shared GPU process), steps 0–6, parameters, open questions.
- **Status:** current for the split, protocol, LOST rule and numbers; partly superseded: S11 (registration and tracking in one GPU process) was replaced by the two-machine split; "Still to do: run on the real camera and arm" is outdated (TN 2026-10-06 evening and 2026-10-09 report real runs); step 5 (FP vs ICP jitter) and `use_roi` remain open; the "GPU host location / model" open questions are not answered in any doc except CAGO (RTX 5070 Ti).
- **Decisions/numbers:** priority = speed (rate, delay); targets 15 Hz min / 30 Hz goal, ≤ 100 ms / ≤ 50 ms delay; achieved 25–29 Hz, 70–90 ms raw (~100 ms with JPEG/PNG), re-seed 4.0 s; 2 frames in flight (1 halved the rate at the same delay); WebSocket over HTTP/gRPC/ROS 2-over-Tailscale.
- **Feeds:** 3.5.2, 3.10.1 (fig:tracking_split), 5.2, 6.5, 6.7.
@@END
@S 3.5.2 | Why a separate tracking mode: `register` searches hundreds of guesses (~1–2 s); `track_one` starts from the last pose and only nudges it with a couple of refiner passes on the GPU ("milliseconds instead of seconds") — RF §In simple terms
@S 3.5.2 | Targets: pose rate in RViz ≥ 15 Hz (goal 30 Hz, the camera's rate); delay image stamp → TF ≤ 100 ms (goal ≤ 50 ms); every stage measured (server timings in each reply: receive, decode, GPU, send; client adds image stamp, sent, reply) — RF §Targets, §Where the time goes
@S 3.5.2 | S1: measure `track_one` alone on both GPUs before writing any transport ("if the laptop tracks at ≥ 20 Hz by itself, there is no network in the loop at all, and that beats any transport"); registration (scorer + ~250 hypotheses) stays on the host — RF S1
@S 3.5.2 | S2: tracking state lives on the server (`est.pose_last`); client streams without waiting; up to 2 frames in flight hide the round trip; the server keeps only the newest waiting frame ("latest wins") → rate limited by the GPU, no backlog — RF S2
@S 3.5.2 | S3: one persistent binary WebSocket (small JSON header: seq, stamp, K, depth scale, ROI, `T_base_cam`; then RGB bytes, then depth bytes; reply JSON: seq, stamp, pose 4×4, fit, timings); not HTTP (one request in flight, multipart parsing, Flask `threaded=False` writing files per request), not gRPC (code generation for no gain), not ROS 2 across Tailscale (DDS discovery; raw 1280×720 RGB-D ~4.6 MB/frame, ~140 MB/s at 30 Hz); fallback ZeroMQ `CONFLATE`; the same protocol works on localhost — RF S3
@S 3.5.2 | S4 ROI crop (`use_roi`, OFF by default): FoundationPose looks only at a square centred on the projected pose, radius = f·(mesh diameter × 1.2 / 2)/z, resampled to 160×160; the client crops to that square + a motion margin (min ~40 px) and shifts K; 5–20× fewer bytes; full frame when lost — RF S4, Status
@S 3.5.2 | S5/S6: remote encoding JPEG q90 RGB + lossless PNG level-1 depth ("never JPEG depth"); local raw bytes; server decodes in memory, writes nothing; try `track_one(iteration=1)` vs 2 only if still-part jitter does not grow; decimate the CAD for rendering; `debug=0`, amp/fp16, cudnn.benchmark — RF S5, S6
@S 3.5.2 | S7 ego-motion cancellation (eye-in-hand): each frame carries `T_base_cam` from TF at the image stamp; before `track_one` the server predicts pose_pred = inv(T_base_cam_now)·T_base_cam_prev·pose_prev, so the tracker sees only the part's own motion; gotcha: FoundationPose stores `pose_last` in its centred-mesh frame (returns `pose_last @ tf_to_centered_mesh`; seed with `pose_last = T_cam_obj @ inv(tf_to_centered_mesh)`) — RF S7
@S 3.5.2 | S8: per frame TF `camera_color_optical_frame → fp_object` stamped with the IMAGE stamp and `/perception/fp/pose`; once a latched MESH_RESOURCE marker with `frame_locked: true` (no point cloud per frame); a status topic (rate, delay, fit, state) — RF S8
@S 3.5.2 | S9 LOST detection: `track_one` returns no score, so the server renders the CAD's depth at the new pose (in the 160×160 crop) and computes fit = fraction of rendered pixels whose measured depth is within 10 mm; fit < 0.5 for 3 frames → LOST, stop publishing TF, automatic re-seed with a mask from the CAD projected at the last good pose (dilated) → `register()` on the newest frame, no click; a SAM2 click is the fallback — RF S9
@S 3.5.2 | S10: one part tracked at a time (the one being placed; saved ones are static); several later = one FP instance per part sharing networks (cost linear) — RF S10
@S 3.5.2 | S11 (design, superseded by the split): one GPU process for registration and tracking with a GPU lock; tracking seedable from the ICP pose or a saved part — RF S11
@S 3.5.2 | Hand-over (step 6, wired 2026-10-06): `pointcloud.launch.py` starts `fp_tracker_node` (`tracking_source:=fp` default); the ICP node idles its timer, takes `/perception/fp/pose` into base_link with TF at each image stamp, averages the frames at rest at `save_object`, stops the tracker after a save and resumes on the next registration; the bridge stamps detections with the registered frame's stamp so the seed's TF is exact; `refine_pose` stays the final stationary step — RF Status, step 6
@S 3.5.2 | Later options if targets were missed: TensorRT for the refiner (NVIDIA Isaac ROS FoundationPose ships TensorRT tracking as a ROS 2 node); several parts at once — RF §Later
@S 5.2 | FoundationPose dependencies for a local install: nvdiffrast, pytorch3d, the `mycpp` extension vs local torch 2.12; otherwise the FoundationPose Docker image on the laptop (NVIDIA container runtime) — the latter was used ("same Docker image") — RF S1, Status
@S 5.1 | Camera node publishes 1280×720 RGB and aligned depth from Python at 30 Hz (to check with `ros2 topic hz`); 640×480 is the fallback — RF §Open questions
